We can compute normalized x once. Use Triton LN:
```
@triton.jit
def layernorm_kernel(x_ptr, w_ptr, b_ptr, y_ptr, M, C: tl.constexpr, eps, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
  pid = tl.program_id(0)
  offs_c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
  mask = offs_c < C
  x = tl.load(x_ptr + pid*C + offs_c, mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
  mean = sum(x)/C
  x_c = tl.where(mask, x - mean, 0)
  var = sum(x_c*x_c)/C
  rstd = 1/sqrt(var+eps)
  w = tl.load(...)
  b = tl.load(...)
  y = (x_c * rstd) * w + b
  tl.store(y_ptr + pid*C + offs_c, y.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=mask)
```
Need sum over C. `tl.sum(x)` supported. C in {128,384,768? input dim maybe 128/384? constraints c {128} but tests dim 384, so C=128/384. Could C=768? Maybe. BLOCK_C power of two >=C: 128,512,1024. For C=384 BLOCK_C=512. This kernel writes M*C bf16. Good. Use `num_warps` maybe 4. M can be large; grid 1M CTAs? Too many small CTAs? Each CTA handles one row (M). Fused projection kernel outputs left/right/out_gate in [H, M] bf16. `M` may be large. Need grid = cdiv(M, BLOCK_M). Use `tl.program_id(0)`. In kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def fused_proj_ln_kernel(
    x_ptr, mask_ptr, 
    wl_ptr, wr_ptr, wlg_ptr, wrg_ptr, wog_ptr,
    lnw_ptr, lnb_ptr,
    left_ptr, right_ptr, og_ptr,
    M, C: tl.constexpr, H: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    offs_m = pid * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    offs_c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    c_mask = offs_c < C
    # load x [BM, BC]
    x_ptrs = x_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :]
    x = tl.load(x_ptrs, mask=m_mask[:, None] & c_mask[None, :], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    inv_c = 1.0 / C
    mean = tl.sum(x, axis=1) * inv_c
    x_centered = tl.where(c_mask[None,:], x - mean[:,None], 0.0)
    var = tl.sum(x_centered * x_centered, axis=1) * inv_c
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)
    ln_w = tl.load(ln_w_ptr + offs_c, mask=c_mask, other=0.0)
    ln_b = tl.load(ln_b_ptr + offs_c, mask=c_mask, other=0.0)
    xn = x_centered * rstd[:, None] * ln_w[None, :] + ln_b[None, :]
    xn = tl.where(c_mask[None,:], xn, 0.0).to(tl.bfloat16)
```
Need `C` integer runtime? tl.constexpr if static. We can pass C as tl.constexpr (host int) to avoid division vector. Since multiple shapes, compile variants.

For each projection, need `yn = dot(xn, trans(W))`. Write helper? In Triton can't define nested @jit? Can define separate @triton.jit functions? In final code, can define @triton.jit helper? Need pointers to weights different; could pass weight pointers as arrays? Triton doesn't have arrays of pointers easily. Could duplicate code for 5 projections.     h_mask = offs_h < H

```
if HAS_MASK:
    mask = tl.load(mask_ptr + offs_m, mask=m_mask, other=0.0) # fp32 maybe

left = tl.where(h_mask[None,:], left, 0.0)
```

Activation kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def activation_kernel(all_ptr, mask_ptr, M, H: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr, HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    offs_m = pid*BM + arange(BM)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    for h0 in range(0, H, BH): # H constexpr? range with runtime? Use tl.static_range if H constexpr and BH constexpr, but H may not constexpr? We pass H constexpr. `for h0 in tl.static_range(0, H, BH)` works if H divisible? If H not multiple, need mask. Static range requires known at compile. H constexpr yes.
       offs_h = h0 + arange(BH)
       h_mask = offs_h < H
       # pointer offsets: row = h, col m
       base = offs_h[:,None]*M + offs_m[None,:]
       mask2d = h_mask[:,None] & m_mask[None,:]
       l = tl.load(all_ptr + base, mask=mask2d, other=0).to(tl.float32)
       r = tl.load(all_ptr + M*H + base? Actually all_ptr + (H+offs_h)*M + m).to(fp32)
       lg = tl.load(all_ptr + (H*2+offs_h)*M + m)
       rg = tl.load(all_ptr + (H*3+offs_h)*M + m)
       og = tl.load(all_ptr + (H*4+offs_h)*M + m)
       if HAS_MASK:
          msk = tl.load(mask_ptr + offs_m, mask=m_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32) # [BM]
          l = l * msk[None,:]
          r = r * msk[None,:]
       l * sigmoid(lg); r * sigmoid(rg); og = sigmoid(og)
       # store first 3H rows
       tl.store(all_ptr + base, l.to(bf16), mask=mask2d)
       tl.store(all_ptr + H*M + base, r.to(bf16), mask=mask2d)
       tl.store(all_ptr + 2*H*M + base, og.to(bf16), mask=mask2d)
```
Need H,M runtime. `H` can be constexpr for static_range. If H=128, good. If not divisible by BH, static_range(0,H,BH) with H constexpr and maybe H%BH !=0? static range supports? Stable sigmoid helper in Triton. Can define:

@triton.jit
def stable_sigmoid(x):
    x_neg_abs = tl.exp(-tl.abs(x))
    return tl.where(x >= 0, 1.0 / (1.0 + x_neg_abs), x_neg_abs / (1.0 + x_neg_abs))
```

```

```
1. `tmp = torch.mm(W_left, xn.t())` [H,M] (bf16)
2. `tmp2 = torch.mm(W_leftgate, xn.t())` [H,M]
3. Activation kernel combine tmp (raw left) and tmp2 (gate) -> left output, reading tmp/tmp2, writing left. Then free tmp/tmp2? They can be re-used? We can allocate `tmp` and `tmp_gate` [H,M], after activation `del`. Peak output left H + tmp 2H =3H.
4. Right similarly.
5. out_gate: `og = torch.mm(W_og, xn.t())`; activation sigmoid in-place -> og.
This requires 5 GEMMs (same FLOPs) but less peak memory (3H vs 5H) and activation kernels separate (more traffic? It reads raw and gate for each output, writes output. For each H, left raw+gate read 2H, write H; right 2H+H; og H read+write H? Total read 6H, write 3H =9H vs combined all path read 5H, write 3H =8H? Slightly more, but memory lower. Let's write final two-pass with `BLOCK_H=128`, `BLOCK_M` heuristic, `BLOCK_C=next_power_of_2(C)` (<=512 for tests). If C >512, maybe fallback PyTorch final. We can conditional.

Need final kernel uses `H` float for division. `H` constexpr. For first pass, sum and sumsq can be large. Use fp32.

Pseudo one-pass:
```
@triton.jit
def final_one_pass_kernel(out_ptr, og_ptr, w_ptr, lnw_ptr, lnb_ptr, out_final_ptr,
                          local_M, B, H: tl.constexpr, C: tl.constexpr,
                          BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
    pid_m=tl.program_id(0); b=tl.program_id(1).to(tl.int64)
    offs_m_local = pid_m*BLOCK_M + arange(BM)
    m_mask = offs_m_local < local_M
    full_m = b*local_M + offs_m_local
    row_stride = B.to(tl.int64)*local_M.to(tl.int64)
    base = b*local_M + offs_m_local
    offs_h = arange(BH); h_mask = offs_h < H
    ptr = (offs_h.to(int64)[:,None]*row_stride + base[None,:])
    mask = h_mask[:,None] & m_mask[None,:]
    x = tl.load(out_ptr + ptr, mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    g = tl.load(og_ptr + ptr, mask=mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    Hf = tl.cast(H, tl.float32)
    s = tl.sum(x, axis=0)
    mean = s / Hf
    xc = tl.where(h_mask[None,:], x - mean[None,:], 0.0)
    var = tl.sum(xc*xc, axis=0) / Hf
    var = tl.where(var < 0, 0, var)
    rstd = 1/tl.sqrt(var+1e-5)
    lnw = tl.load(lnw_ptr + offs_h, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
    lnb = tl.load(lnb_ptr + offs_h, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
    y = xc * (rstd[None,:] * lnw[None,:]) + lnb[None,:] * tl.where(h_mask[None,:],1,0)
    # for padded h, lnb maybe 0 (loaded other=0), where 0 so y 0.
    y = y * g
    yt = tl.trans(y).to(tl.bfloat16)
    offs_c = arange(BC); c_mask=offs_c<C
    w = tl.load(w_ptr + offs_c[:,None]*H + offs_h[None,:], mask=c_mask[:,None]&h_mask[None,:], other=0.0).to(tl.bfloat16)
    wt = tl.trans(w)
    acc = tl.dot(yt, wt)
    # store
    out_ptrs = out_final_ptr + full_m[:,None]*C + offs_c[None,:]
    tl.store(out_ptrs, acc.to(tl.float32), mask=m_mask[:,None]&c_mask[None,:])
```
Note `base` for out/og same. But `full_m` final uses b*local_M+offs. `base` same as full_m. row_stride = B*local_M. `offs_h * row_stride + base`. Since out3 p = h*B+b; address = (h*B+b)*local_M + m. = h*B*local_M + b*local_M + m. yes. og address same.

 Need `tl.cast(H, tl.float32)` maybe H Python int; `float(H)` host? In kernel, `Hf = float(H)`? Triton allows Python float literal. Use `Hf = H.to(tl.float32)`? H constexpr scalar; `tl.cast(H, tl.float32)` works? Could just use `H` in division: `s / H` where H int, promotes to float? In Triton, division by int probably okay, but to be safe: `Hf = tl.cast(H, tl.float32)`. For C in pointer offset, use int.

`tl.where(h_mask[None,:], x - mean[None,:], 0.0)` returns fp32. For valid h, okay.

`y = y * g` where g padded 0 (loaded other 0). Good.

Potential issue: If H not power of two, Hf = H actual. Sum over valid rows uses padded zeros, okay. Var uses xc masked for padded. Good.

Two-pass:
```
@triton.jit
def final_two_pass_kernel(...):
  pid_m, b
  offs, masks, ptrs base, row_stride
  sum = zeros(BM); sumsq = zeros(BM)
  for h0 in range(0,H,BH):
     offs_h = h0 + arange(BH); hmask
     ptr = offs_h.to(int64)[:,None]*row_stride + base[None,:]
     mask = hmask[:,None] & m_mask[None,:]
     x = load out...
     sum += tl.sum(x, axis=0)
     sumsq += tl.sum(x*x, axis=0)
  mean = sum/Hf; var = sumsq/Hf - mean*mean; clamp; rstd
  acc = zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), fp32)
  loop h0:
     load x, g
     xc = where(hmask, x-mean,0)
     y=...
     yt=trans(y).to(bf16)
     offs_c; w_chunk = load w_ptr + offs_c[:,None]*H + (h0+offs_h)[None,:]
     wt=trans(w)
     acc += tl.dot(yt, wt)
  store
```
Need for W chunk c axis full BC. For H chunks, h0 runtime? H constexpr; `h0 + arange` int. Use int64 for offsets.

`range(0, H, BH)` inside JIT: H and BH constexpr, but if we use ordinary for, `h0` is Python int? In JIT trace, it may create loop. If H not multiple, last chunk hmask. Good.

`acc += tl.dot(...)`; Triton supports in-place add? Use `acc = acc + dot` or `acc +=`. In example they use `acc_1 += tl.dot(...)`. okay.

Now, if C<=512 use BLOCK_C=next_power_of_2(C). If C=384, BC=512. If C=128, BC=128. If C=768, fallback to PyTorch final or use BC=1024. Let's set if `C <= 512 and (H <= 128 or True)`: use final Triton one/two. For C>512, fallback. Also if H >? two-pass supports any H. Good.

What if M < BLOCK_M? m_mask. Grid cdiv. For B dimension program_id(1) works.

Now, host selection for final:
```
if C <= 512:
   use_one = H <= 128
   if use_one: BLOCK_H=triton.next_power_of_2(H); BLOCK_M=...; BLOCK_C=triton.next_power_of_2(C)
   else: BLOCK_H=128; BLOCK_M=...; BLOCK_C=triton.next_power_of_2(C)
   grid=(triton.cdiv(local_M, BLOCK_M), B)
   kernel[grid](out3, og, w_out, lnw_out, lnb_out, output, local_M, B, H=H, C=C, BLOCK_M=..., BLOCK_H=..., BLOCK_C=..., num_warps=4)
else:
   fallback_pytorch_final(...)
```
For H=128, BLOCK_H=128. For H=64, next_power 64. For H=384, use two-pass BLOCK_H=128.

Need `w_out` bf16 [C,H] for Triton final. For fallback, use float32 `w_out_f32`. We can cast once: `w_out = weights['to_out.weight'].to(torch.bfloat16).contiguous()`. For fallback use `.float()`. Or `w_out_f32 = weights['to_out.weight'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()`.

```
out_b = out3.view(H, B, N, N).permute(1,2,3,0).contiguous().float()
og_b = og.view(H, B, N, N).permute(1,2,3,0).contiguous().float()
out_b = F.layer_norm(out_b, (H,), lnw_out, lnb_out, 1e-5)
out_b = out_b * og_b
out = F.linear(out_b, w_out_f32)

```

Activation kernel code with H chunks:
```
@triton.jit
def activation_kernel(all_ptr, mask_ptr, M, H: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr, HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr):
    pid=tl.program_id(0)
    offs_m = (pid*BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_M)).to(tl.int64)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    if HAS_MASK:
       msk = tl.load(mask_ptr + offs_m, mask=m_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    else:
       msk = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M), dtype=tl.float32) # not used
    for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
       offs_h = (h0 + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_H)).to(tl.int64)
       h_mask = offs_h < H
       base = offs_h[:,None]*M + offs_m[None,:]
       mask2d = h_mask[:,None] & m_mask[None,:]
       l = tl.load(all_ptr + base, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
       r = tl.load(all_ptr + H*M + base? Need H as scalar; expression `H*M + base`? H constexpr, M runtime int. Could compute `H_M = H.to(tl.int64)*M.to(tl.int64)`? In kernel, H is constexpr int, M runtime. Use `H_M = tl.cast(H, tl.int64) * tl.cast(M, tl.int64)`. Similarly row offsets.
```
Let's compute offsets carefully:
```
M_i64 = tl.cast(M, tl.int64)

left = tl.load(all_ptr + base, ...)


LN kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def layernorm_kernel(x_ptr, lnw_ptr, lnb_ptr, y_ptr, M, C: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    pid64 = tl.cast(pid, tl.int64)
    offs_m = pid64 * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M).to(tl.int64)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    offs_c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    c_mask = offs_c < C
    ptrs = x_ptr + offs_m[:,None]*C + offs_c[None,:]
    x = tl.load(ptrs, mask=m_mask[:,None] & c_mask[None,:], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    inv_c = 1.0 / tl.cast(C, tl.float32)
    mean = tl.sum(x, axis=1) * inv_c
    sumsq = tl.sum(x*x, axis=1) * inv_c
    var = sumsq - mean*mean
    var = tl.where(var < 0.0, 0.0, var)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)
    lnw = tl.load(lnw_ptr + offs_c, mask=c_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    lnb = ...
    y = (x - mean[:,None]) * (rstd[:,None] * lnw[None,:]) + lnb[None,:]
    y = tl.where(c_mask[None,:], y, 0.0)
    tl.store(y_ptr + offs_m[:,None]*C + offs_c[None,:], y.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=...)
```
If C not power of two, BLOCK_C padded. y for padded c set 0. Good.

`C` as constexpr in pointer offset `offs_m[:,None]*C + offs_c`: offs_m int64, C int. Good.

```
@triton.jit
def fused_proj_ln_kernel(x_ptr, mask_ptr, wl_ptr, wr_ptr, wlg_ptr, wrg_ptr, wog_ptr, lnw_ptr, lnb_ptr, proj_ptr, M, HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr):
    pid= program_id(0); pid64=...
    offs_m = pid64*BLOCK_M + arange(BLOCK_M).to(int64)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    offs_c = tl.arange(0,128)
    offs_h = tl.arange(0,128)
    x = tl.load(x_ptr + offs_m[:,None]*128 + offs_c[None,:], mask=m_mask[:,None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    inv = 1.0/128.0
    mean=...
    xn=...
    xt=tl.trans(xn)
    # left
    w=tl.load(wl_ptr + offs_h[:,None]*128 + offs_c[None,:])
    left=tl.dot(w, xt)
    w=tl.load(wlg_ptr + ...)
    lg=tl.dot(w, xt)
    # sigmoid
    exp_neg=tl.exp(-tl.abs(lg))
    lgate=tl.where(lg>=0, 1/(1+exp_neg), exp_neg/(1+exp_neg))
    left = left * lgate
    if HAS_MASK:
       msk = tl.load(mask_ptr + offs_m, mask=m_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
       left = left * msk[None,:]
    store left: tl.store(proj_ptr + offs_h[:,None]*M_i64 + offs_m[None,:], left.to(bf16), mask=m_mask[None,:])
```

One concern: In Triton, using `if HAS_MASK:` inside kernel with `HAS_MASK` tl.constexpr is allowed. Yes.

Another: `for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):` where H and BLOCK_H are `tl.constexpr`. In Triton JIT, the `range` is a Python built-in during tracing. Since H is a constexpr, it will unroll. If H not a Python int at trace? It is. Good. But if `H` is large, unrolling many loops (H/128 up to 6) fine. For C chunks up to6. Good.

Another: `tl.cast(H, tl.int64)` inside kernel where H is a pipe? It should. If unsure, use `H64 = H` and `M64 = tl.cast(M, tl.int64)`; Python int * int64 scalar maybe promotes. For row offset `4*H*M64`, Python computes `4*H` int then multiply by int64, okay. In kernel, `M64` scalar. No need cast H. For final row_stride: `B` and `local_M` are scalar args; if not cast, might be int32. Use `local_M64 = tl.cast(local_M, tl.int64); B64 = tl.cast(B, tl.int64)`. For H, not multiplied with local_M? In W offset `offs_c[:,None] * H`, offs_c int64, H Python -> int64. In activation base, `offs_h[:,None] * M64`, H row offset Python*`M64`. Good. In activation row offsets: `H_M = H * M64`; `2H_M = (2 * H) * M64`; H Python int, M64 int64. Good. Avoid `tl.cast(H)`. Is `H` a `tl.constexpr` object; multiplication with M64 okay. Yes.

For LN `C` in pointer offset: `offs_m[:,None] * C`; C Python int? `C` is tl.constexpr. offs_m int64, product int64. Good.

```python
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
import triton
import triton.language as tl


def _next_pow2(n: int) -> int:
    p = 1
    while p < n:
        p *= 2
    return p


@triton.jit
def layernorm_kernel(
    x_ptr,
    lnw_ptr,
    lnb_ptr,
    y_ptr,
    M,
    C: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    pid64 = tl.cast(pid, tl.int64)

    offs_m = pid64 * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M).to(tl.int64)
    offs_c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)

    m_mask = offs_m < M
    c_mask = offs_c < C
    mask2d = m_mask[:, None] & c_mask[None, :]

    ptrs = x_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :]
    x = tl.load(ptrs, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    inv_c = 1.0 / float(C)
    mean = tl.sum(x, axis=1) * inv_c
    var = tl.sum(x * x, axis=1) * inv_c - mean * mean
    var = tl.where(var < 0.0, 0.0, var)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)

    lnw = tl.load(lnw_ptr + offs_c, mask=c_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    lnb = tl.load(lnb_ptr + offs_c, mask=c_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    y = (x - mean[:, None]) * (rstd[:, None] * lnw[None, :]) + lnb[None, :]
    y = tl.where(c_mask[None, :], y, 0.0)

    tl.store(y_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :], y.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=mask2d)


@triton.jit
def fused_ln_proj_kernel(
    x_ptr,
    mask_ptr,
    wl_ptr,
    wr_ptr,
    wlg_ptr,
    wrg_ptr,
    wog_ptr,
    lnw_ptr,
    lnb_ptr,
    proj_ptr,
    M,
    HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
):
    # Specialized fast path: C == 128, H == 128.
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    pid64 = tl.cast(pid, tl.int64)

    offs_m = pid64 * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M).to(tl.int64)
    m_mask = offs_m < M

    offs_c = tl.arange(0, 128)
    offs_h = tl.arange(0, 128)

    # Load input block [BLOCK_M, 128]
    x_ptrs = x_ptr + offs_m[:, None] * 128 + offs_c[None, :]
    x = tl.load(x_ptrs, mask=m_mask[:, None], other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    # LayerNorm over C=128
    inv_c = 1.0 / 128.0
    mean = tl.sum(x, axis=1) * inv_c
    var = tl.sum(x * x, axis=1) * inv_c - mean * mean
    var = tl.where(var < 0.0, 0.0, var)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)

    lnw = tl.load(lnw_ptr + offs_c).to(tl.float32)
    lnb = tl.load(lnb_ptr + offs_c).to(tl.float32)

    xn = ((x - mean[:, None]) * (rstd[:, None] * lnw[None, :]) + lnb[None, :]).to(tl.bfloat16)
    xt = tl.trans(xn)  # [128, BLOCK_M]

    M64 = tl.cast(M, tl.int64)

    # left = xn @ W_left.T  ->  [H, M]
    w = tl.load(wl_ptr + offs_h[:, None] * 128 + offs_c[None, :])
    left = tl.dot(w, xt)

    # left_gate
    w = tl.load(wlg_ptr + offs_h[:, None] * 128 + offs_c[None, :])
    lg = tl.dot(w, xt)
    e = tl.exp(-tl.abs(lg))
    lgate = tl.where(lg >= 0, 1.0 / (1.0 + e), e / (1.0 + e))
    left = left * lgate

    if HAS_MASK:
        msk = tl.load(mask_ptr + offs_m, mask=m_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        left = left * msk[None, :]

    tl.store(
        proj_ptr + offs_h[:, None] * M64 + offs_m[None, :],
        left.to(tl.bfloat16),
        mask=m_mask[None, :],
    )

    # right = xn @ W_right.T  ->  [H, M]
    w = tl.load(wr_ptr + offs_h[:, None] * 128 + offs_c[None, :])
    right = tl.dot(w, xt)

    # right_gate
    w = tl.load(wrg_ptr + offs_h[:, None] * 128 + offs_c[None, :])
    rg = tl.dot(w, xt)
    e = tl.exp(-tl.abs(rg))
    rgate = tl.where(rg >= 0, 1.0 / (1.0 + e), e / (1.0 + e))
    right = right * rgate

    if HAS_MASK:
        msk = tl.load(mask_ptr + offs_m, mask=m_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        right = right * msk[None, :]

    tl.store(
        proj_ptr + 128 * M64 + offs_h[:, None] * M64 + offs_m[None, :],
        right.to(tl.bfloat16),
        mask=m_mask[None, :],
    )

    # out_gate = sigmoid(xn @ W_out_gate.T)  ->  [H, M]
    w = tl.load(wog_ptr + offs_h[:, None] * 128 + offs_c[None, :])
    og = tl.dot(w, xt)
    e = tl.exp(-tl.abs(og))
    og = tl.where(og >= 0, 1.0 / (1.0 + e), e / (1.0 + e))

    tl.store(
        proj_ptr + 256 * M64 + offs_h[:, None] * M64 + offs_m[None, :],
        og.to(tl.bfloat16),
        mask=m_mask[None, :],
    )


@triton.jit
def activate_kernel(
    all_ptr,
    mask_ptr,
    M,
    H: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
):
    # all layout: [5H, M]
    # row 0:H       left raw
    # row H:2H      right raw
    # row 2H:3H     left_gate raw
    # row 3H:4H     right_gate raw
    # row 4H:5H     out_gate raw
    # After this kernel:
    # row 0:H       left gated
    # row H:2H      right gated
    # row 4H:5H     out_gate sigmoid
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    pid64 = tl.cast(pid, tl.int64)

    offs_m = pid64 * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M).to(tl.int64)
    m_mask = offs_m < M
    M64 = tl.cast(M, tl.int64)

    H_M = H * M64
    2H_M = (2 * H) * M64
    3H_M = (3 * H) * M64
    4H_M = (4 * H) * M64

    if HAS_MASK:
        msk = tl.load(mask_ptr + offs_m, mask=m_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
        offs_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H).to(tl.int64) + h0
        h_mask = offs_h < H
        base = offs_h[:, None] * M64 + offs_m[None, :]
        mask2d = h_mask[:, None] & m_mask[None, :]

        left = tl.load(all_ptr + base, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        right = tl.load(all_ptr + H_M + base, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        lg = tl.load(all_ptr + 2H_M + base, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        rg = tl.load(all_ptr + 3H_M + base, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        og = tl.load(all_ptr + 4H_M + base, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

        if HAS_MASK:
            left = left * msk[None, :]
            right = right * msk[None, :]

        e = tl.exp(-tl.abs(lg))
        lgate = tl.where(lg >= 0, 1.0 / (1.0 + e), e / (1.0 + e))
        left = left * lgate

        e = tl.exp(-tl.abs(rg))
        rgate = tl.where(rg >= 0, 1.0 / (1.0 + e), e / (1.0 + e))
        right = right * rgate

        e = tl.exp(-tl.abs(og))
        og = tl.where(og >= 0, 1.0 / (1.0 + e), e / (1.0 + e))

        tl.store(all_ptr + base, left.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=mask2d)
        tl.store(all_ptr + H_M + base, right.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=mask2d)
        tl.store(all_ptr + 4H_M + base, og.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=mask2d)


@triton.jit
def final_one_pass_kernel(
    out_ptr,
    og_ptr,
    w_ptr,
    lnw_ptr,
    lnb_ptr,
    out_final_ptr,
    local_M,
    B,
    H: tl.constexpr,
    C: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
):
    # One-pass final fusion: out3 [H*B, N, N], og [H, B*N*N]
    # This path is used when H <= 128, and loops over C in chunks of BLOCK_C.
    pid_m = tl.program_id(0)
    b = tl.program_id(1)

    pid_m64 = tl.cast(pid_m, tl.int64)
    b64 = tl.cast(b, tl.int64)
    local_M64 = tl.cast(local_M, tl.int64)

    offs_m_local = pid_m64 * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M).to(tl.int64)
    m_mask = offs_m_local < local_M

    # Full flattened position in output: b * local_M + m
    full_m = b64 * local_M64 + offs_m_local
    row_stride = b64 * 0 + tl.cast(B, tl.int64) * local_M64  # B * local_M

    offs_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H)
    h_mask = offs_h < H

    ptr = offs_h[:, None] * row_stride + full_m[None, :]
    mask2d = h_mask[:, None] & m_mask[None, :]

    x = tl.load(out_ptr + ptr, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    g = tl.load(og_ptr + ptr, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    inv_h = 1.0 / float(H)
    mean = tl.sum(x, axis=0) * inv_h
    xc = tl.where(h_mask[None, :], x - mean[None, :], 0.0)
    var = tl.sum(xc * xc, axis=0) * inv_h
    var = tl.where(var < 0.0, 0.0, var)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)

    lnw = tl.load(lnw_ptr + offs_h, mask=h_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    lnb = tl.load(lnb_ptr + offs_h, mask=h_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    shift = tl.where(h_mask[None, :], lnb[None, :], 0.0)
    y = xc * (rstd[None, :] * lnw[None, :]) + shift
    y = y * g

    yt = tl.trans(y).to(tl.bfloat16)  # [BLOCK_M, BLOCK_H]

    for c0 in range(0, C, BLOCK_C):
        offs_c = c0 + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
        c_mask = offs_c < C

        w_ptrs = w_ptr + offs_c[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :]
        w = tl.load(w_ptrs, mask=c_mask[:, None] & h_mask[None, :], other=0.0).to(tl.bfloat16)
        acc = tl.dot(yt, tl.trans(w))

        out_ptrs = out_final_ptr + full_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :]
        tl.store(out_ptrs, acc.to(tl.float32), mask=m_mask[:, None] & c_mask[None, :])


@triton.jit
def final_two_pass_kernel(
    out_ptr,
    og_ptr,
    w_ptr,
    lnw_ptr,
    lnb_ptr,
    out_final_ptr,
    local_M,
    B,
    H: tl.constexpr,
    C: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_H: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,
):
    # Two-pass final fusion for H > 128. This path is used when C <= 128.
    pid_m = tl.program_id(0)
    b = tl.program_id(1)

    pid_m64 = tl.cast(pid_m, tl.int64)
    b64 = tl.cast(b, tl.int64)
    local_M64 = tl.cast(local_M, tl.int64)

    offs_m_local = pid_m64 * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M).to(tl.int64)
    m_mask = offs_m_local < local_M

    full_m = b64 * local_M64 + offs_m_local
    row_stride = tl.cast(B, tl.int64) * local_M64

    inv_h = 1.0 / float(H)

    sum_x = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
    sum_sq = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)

    # First pass: mean/var
    for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
        offs_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H).to(tl.int64) + h0
        h_mask = offs_h < H
        ptr = offs_h[:, None] * row_stride + full_m[None, :]
        mask2d = h_mask[:, None] & m_mask[None, :]
        x = tl.load(out_ptr + ptr, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        sum_x += tl.sum(x, axis=0)
        sum_sq += tl.sum(x * x, axis=0)

    mean = sum_x * inv_h
    var = sum_sq * inv_h - mean * mean
    var = tl.where(var < 0.0, 0.0, var)
    rstd = 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + 1e-5)

    # Second pass: normalize, gate, and project
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)

    for h0 in range(0, H, BLOCK_H):
        offs_h = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_H).to(tl.int64) + h0
        h_mask = offs_h < H
        ptr = offs_h[:, None] * row_stride + full_m[None, :]
        mask2d = h_mask[:, None] & m_mask[None, :]

        x = tl.load(out_ptr + ptr, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        g = tl.load(og_ptr + ptr, mask=mask2d, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

        lnw = tl.load(lnw_ptr + offs_h, mask=h_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        lnb = tl.load(lnb_ptr + offs_h, mask=h_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

        xc = tl.where(h_mask[None, :], x - mean[None, :], 0.0)
        shift = tl.where(h_mask[None, :], lnb[None, :], 0.0)
        y = xc * (rstd[None, :] * lnw[None, :]) + shift
        y = y * g

        yt = tl.trans(y).to(tl.bfloat16)

        offs_c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
        c_mask = offs_c < C
        w = tl.load(
            w_ptr + offs_c[:, None] * H + offs_h[None, :],
            mask=c_mask[:, None] & h_mask[None, :],
            other=0.0,
        ).to(tl.bfloat16)
        acc = acc + tl.dot(yt, tl.trans(w))

    offs_c = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    c_mask = offs_c < C
    out_ptrs = out_final_ptr + full_m[:, None] * C + offs_c[None, :]
    tl.store(out_ptrs, acc.to(tl.float32), mask=m_mask[:, None] & c_mask[None, :])


def custom_kernel(data):
    """
    TriMul forward:
      1) LayerNorm + five projections + mask/gates (Triton for the common C=H=128 case,
         otherwise PyTorch GEMM + Triton activation).
      2) Batched TriMul core: out[p=i,j,h] = sum_k left[p,i,k,h] * right[p,j,k,h]
         via cuBLAS batched matrix multiplication.
      3) Final LayerNorm + out_gate + projection (Triton when practical, otherwise PyTorch).
    Output is float32.
    """
    x, mask, weights, config = data

    B = x.shape[0]
    N = x.shape[1]
    C = x.shape[3]
    H = int(config.get("hidden_dim", C))

    M = B * N * N
    local_M = N * N
    device = x.device

    if not x.is_contiguous():
        x = x.contiguous()
    x2d = x.view(M, C)

    try:
        nomask = bool(config.get("nomask", False))
    except Exception:
        nomask = False

    if mask is not None and not nomask:
        mask_flat = mask.reshape(-1).to(torch.float32, device=device)
        if not mask_flat.is_contiguous():
            mask_flat = mask_flat.contiguous()
        has_mask = True
    else:
        mask_flat = x2d
        has_mask = False

    ln_w = weights["norm.weight"].to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()
    ln_b = weights["norm.bias"].to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()

    out_ln_w = weights["to_out_norm.weight"].to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()
    out_ln_b = weights["to_out_norm.bias"].to(torch.float32, device=device).contiguous()

    # Fast fully-fused preprocessing path for the common target shape C=128, H=128.
    if C == 128 and H == 128:
        proj = torch.empty((3 * H, M), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=device)

        w_l = weights["left_proj.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device).contiguous()
        w_r = weights["right_proj.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device).contiguous()
        w_lg = weights["left_gate.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device).contiguous()
        w_rg = weights["right_gate.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device).contiguous()
        w_og = weights["out_gate.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device).contiguous()

        BLOCK_M = 64
        grid = (triton.cdiv(M, BLOCK_M),)

        fused_ln_proj_kernel[grid](
            x2d,
            mask_flat,
            w_l,
            w_r,
            w_lg,
            w_rg,
            w_og,
            ln_w,
            ln_b,
            proj,
            M,
            HAS_MASK=has_mask,
            BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M,
            num_warps=4,
        )

        left = proj[:H]
        right = proj[H : 2 * H]
        og = proj[2 * H : 3 * H]

    else:
        # General path: Triton LN -> PyTorch GEMM -> Triton activation.
        BLOCK_C = _next_pow2(C)
        if C <= 128:
            ln_BM = 64
        elif C <= 384:
            ln_BM = 32
        else:
            ln_BM = 16

        xn = torch.empty((M, C), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=device)
        ln_grid = (triton.cdiv(M, ln_BM),)

        layernorm_kernel[ln_grid](
            x2d,
            ln_w,
            ln_b,
            xn,
            M,
            C=C,
            BLOCK_M=ln_BM,
            BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
            num_warps=4,
        )

        Wc = torch.empty((5 * H, C), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=device)
        Wc[0:H].copy_(weights["left_proj.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device))
        Wc[H : 2 * H].copy_(weights["right_proj.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device))
        Wc[2 * H : 3 * H].copy_(weights["left_gate.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device))
        Wc[3 * H : 4 * H].copy_(weights["right_gate.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device))
        Wc[4 * H : 5 * H].copy_(weights["out_gate.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device))

        all_proj = torch.mm(Wc, xn.T())
        del xn

        if H <= 128:
            act_BM = 32
            act_BH = _next_pow2(H)
        else:
            act_BM = 16
            act_BH = 128

        act_grid = (triton.cdiv(M, act_BM),)

        activate_kernel[act_grid](
            all_proj,
            mask_flat,
            M,
            H=H,
            BLOCK_M=act_BM,
            BLOCK_H=act_BH,
            HAS_MASK=has_mask,
            num_warps=4,
        )

        left = all_proj[:H]
        right = all_proj[H : 2 * H]
        og = all_proj[4 * H : 5 * H]

    # Core TriMul:
    # left  [H, B*N*N] -> [H*B, N, N]
    # right [H, B*N*N] -> [H*B, N, N]
    # out = left @ right.T (per p = h*B+b)
    P = H * B
    left3 = left.view(P, N, N)
    right3 = right.view(P, N, N)

    out3 = torch.bmm(left3, right3.transpose(1, 2))

    # Final LayerNorm + gate + projection.
    output = torch.empty((B, N, N, C), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)

    # Use Triton final kernel for the common shapes.
    if H >= 16 and (H <= 128 or C <= 128):
        w_out = weights["to_out.weight"].to(torch.bfloat16, device=device).contiguous()

        if H <= 128:
            BLOCK_H = _next_pow2(H)
            BLOCK_M = 64
            BLOCK_C = 128
            grid = (triton.cdiv(local_M, BLOCK_M), B)

            final_one_pass_kernel[grid](
                out3,
                og,
                w_out,
                out_ln_w,
                out_ln_b,
                output,
                local_M,
                B,
                H=H,
                C=C,
                BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M,
                BLOCK_H=BLOCK_H,
                BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
                num_warps=4,
            )
        else:
            BLOCK_H = 128
            BLOCK_M = 64
            BLOCK_C = 128
            grid = (triton.cdiv(local_M, BLOCK_M), B)

            final_two_pass_kernel[grid](
                out3,
                og,
                w_out,
                out_ln_w,
                out_ln_b,
                output,
                local_M,
                B,
                H=H,
                C=C,
                BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M,
                BLOCK_H=BLOCK_H,
                BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
                num_warps=4,
            )

    else:
        # Fallback final path for less common shapes.
        out_b = out3.reshape(H, B, N, N).permute(1, 2, 3, 0).contiguous().float()
        og_b = og.reshape(H, B, N, N).permute(1, 2, 3, 0).contiguous().float()

        out_b = F.layer_norm(out_b, (H,), out_ln_w, out_ln_b, 1e-5)
        out_b = out_b * og_b

        w_out_f32 = weights["to_out.weight"].to(torch.float32, device=device)
        output = F.linear(out_b, w_out_f32).view(B, N, N, C)

    return output
```
